# D199 complete prospective decision tables

2 November 2026. Generated by `scripts/report_l20_recipient_decision_fresh.py`
from pinned, separately audited outputs. [Interpretation](FRESH_RESULTS.md).
No simulations, model fitting, new uncertainty estimates or post-outcome policy choices.
Preference and differences are fraction ×100 (points), not success percentages.
Correlations, body RMS and clipping remain in native units; guide retention is percent.
The independent unit is the founder block; records/lives/futures are nested.
Action counts are in order 0.75 / 1.0 / 1.25. A/B and writer context are supplied.
Common/native assay names denote the terminal evaluation reader, while the native
policy chooses reconstruction coupling 1.0. The common policy chooses 0.75.

The frozen `benchmark_excluded=false` metadata is misleadingly named; actual
cohort membership excludes disposable 519900. See [metadata note](FRESH_METADATA_NOTE.md).
